Section 0

Cambiar is a fast Rust CLI for converting files between different formats. This paragraph exists to create realistic benchmark data for HTML to Markdown conversion. Performance engineering is easier with representative inputs.

Features

  • Fast conversion
  • Memory efficient
  • Cross-platform
  • Open source
Simplicity is prerequisite for reliability. 

fn main() {
    println!("Hello Cambiar!");
}
NameLanguage
CambiarRust
PandocHaskell

Visit our website for more information.

Section 899

Cambiar is a fast Rust CLI for converting files between different formats. This paragraph exists to create realistic benchmark data for HTML to Markdown conversion. Performance engineering is easier with representative inputs.

Features

  • Fast conversion
  • Memory efficient
  • Cross-platform
  • Open source
Simplicity is prerequisite for reliability.
